Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The boltzmann operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 765. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 930 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the boltzmann space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 765 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 930. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on boltzmann structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 566. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 463 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the boltzmann decomposition. By minimality, the element a^566 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 463-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
